# star_talk — days 26–55 posting plan (doubled)

The entertainment account: **647 posts over 30 days, about 22 a day**. Every video is new and nothing repeats.

- **Sun 29 Nov – Mon 28 Dec** (days 26–55). Times are in your local time.
- The first 900 videos keep their original times. The Month 2 boost sits in between, so the account posts every 45 minutes or so, and Planet Talk has an early and a late slot each day.
- **⏱** marks a long video (1 minute or more). Each account gets several a day, at least one in the evening.
- **Files:** each title links to its video in Google Drive; the path under it shows where it sits in the `Star Talk` folder.
- Captions have no hashtags and no emoji.
